=== function renderTickerCardNow @ 1684135 ===
rTransition(renderFn, immediate) {
  const row = document.getElementById('tickerInnerRow');
  const content = document.getElementById('tickerContent');
  if (!row || !content || immediate || !String(content.textContent || '').trim()) {
    renderFn();
    if (row) {
      row.classList.remove('rotastic-ticker-fade-out');
      row.classList.add('rotastic-ticker-fade-in');
      requestAnimationFrame(function() {
        requestAnimationFrame(function() { row.classList.remove('rotastic-ticker-fade-in'); });
      });
    }
    return;
  }

  if (rotasticTickerTransitionTimer) clearTimeout(rotasticTickerTransitionTimer);
  rotasticTickerTransitioning = true;
  row.classList.remove('rotastic-ticker-fade-in');
  row.classList.add('rotastic-ticker-fade-out');

  rotasticTickerTransitionTimer = setTimeout(function() {
    renderFn();
    row.classList.remove('rotastic-ticker-fade-out');
    row.classList.add('rotastic-ticker-fade-in');
    requestAnimationFrame(function() {
      requestAnimationFrame(function() {
        row.classList.remove('rotastic-ticker-fade-in');
        rotasticTickerTransitioning = false;
      });
    });
    rotasticTickerTransitionTimer = null;
  }, 320);
}

function renderTickerCardNow(type, message, durationMs, fromHistory) {
  rotasticTickerLastRenderAt = Date.now();
  message = String(message || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  const knowledgeType = ['stat','medicalnews','worldnews','qnanews','alarabiyanews','reutersnews','nhsinfo'].includes(type);
  if (knowledgeType) message = fitNormalTickerCopy(message, 16, 112);
  else if (!['sick','centre','greeting','welcome','notification'].includes(type)) message = fitNormalTickerCopy(message, 18, 124) || message;
  if (!message) {
    message = nextDoctorEncouragement() || 'Wishing you a smooth and safe shift today.';
    type = 'encouragement';
  }
  if (rotasticActiveTab !== 'schedule') {
    const hiddenTicker = document.getElementById('swapsNewsTicker');
    if (hiddenTicker) hiddenTicker.classList.add('hidden');
    return;
  }
  const ticker = document.getElementById('swapsNewsTicker');
  const content = document.getElementById('tickerContent');
  if (!ticker || !content) return;
  ticker.classList.remove('ticker-greeting-mode');
  setTickerFullWidthMode(false);
  ticker.classList.remove('hidden');
  content.innerHTML = '';
  setTickerBadge(type, message);
  if (!fromHistory) rememberRotasticTickerItem(type, message);
  const card = document.createElement('div');
  const leftAligned = ['safepractice','encouragement','stat','qatar','qnanews','medicalnews','worldnews','alarabiyanews','reutersnews','nhsinfo','prayer','admin','clinical','rota','general','notification'].includes(type);
  card.className = 'ticker-message-card ' + getTickerCopySizeClass(message) +
    (type === 'rota' ? ' ticker-rota-change-card' : '') +
    (leftAligned ? ' ticker-left-card' : '') +
    (type === 'prayer' ? ' ticker-prayer-card' : '');
  if (type === 'prayer') {
    const body = document.createElement('span');
    body.className = 'ticker-message-text';
    body.textContent = message;
    card.appendChild(body);
  } else if ((type === 'medicalnews' && rotasticCurrentMedicalLink) || (['qnanews','worldnews','alarabiyanews','reutersnews'].includes(type) && rotasticCurrentTickerLink)) {
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.className = 'ticker-medical-link';
    link.href = type === 'medicalnews' ? rotasticCurrentMedicalLink : rotasticCurrentTickerLink;
    link.target = '_blank';
    link.rel = 'noopener noreferrer';
    link.textContent = message;
    link.title = 'Open source';
    card.appendChild(link);
  } else {
    card.textContent = message;
  }
  content.appendChild(card);
  if (headlineTickerTimer) clearTimeout(headlineTickerTimer);
  const requestedDuration = Number(durationMs || 0);
  const comfortableDuration = getTickerDisplayDuration(message, 7800);
  const finalDuration = Math.max(requestedDuration, comfortableDuration);
  headlineTickerTimer = setTimeout(function() { renderNextTickerSlot(); }, finalDuration);
  scheduleSickTakeoverInterrupt();
}


function renderTickerCard(type, message, durationMs, fromHistory) {
  const immediate = type === 'sick' || type === 'greeting' || type === 'welcome';
  rotasticRunTickerTransition(function() {
    renderTickerCardNow(type, message, durationMs, fromHistory);
  }, immediate);
}

function choosePrayerMessage(prayerTimes) {
  const list = getRelevantPrayerMessages(prayerTimes);
  if (!list.length) return '';
  const message = list[prayerTickerIndex % list.length];
  prayerTickerIndex = (prayerTickerIndex + 1) % list.length;
  return message;
}



const ROTASTIC_GREETING_VIBES = [
  'Hope you have a smooth shift today.',
  'Wishing you a calm and safe shift.',
  'Hope today feels steady and manageable.',
  'Have a good shift and remember to hydrate.',
  'Wishing you a positive and uncomplicated day.',
  'Take it one patient at a time today.',
  'Hope the clinic stays calm and kind to you.',
  'Remember to pause, hydrate and reset when you can.',
  'Wishing you clear decisions and an easy workflow.',
  'Hope you get a few quiet moments between patients.',
  'Keep it calm, careful and compassionate today.',
  'Wishing you a safe shift and an easy handover.',
  'Look after yourself while you look after everyone else.',
  'Hope today brings more smiles than surprises.',
  'May your shift be smooth and your coffee stay warm.',
  'Wishing you a steady pace and a good team around you.',
  'Stay hydrated and give yourself a moment to breathe.',
  'Hope the day runs gently from start to finish.',
  'Wishing you good energy for the whole shift.',
  'Have a safe shift and be kind to yourself too.'
];

let rotasticLastGreetingVibe = '';

function nextGreetingVibe() {
  if (!ROTASTIC_GREETING_VIBES.length) return '';

  let options = ROTASTIC_GREETING_VIBES.filter(function(msg) {
    return msg !== rotasticLastGreetingVibe;
  });

  if (!options.length) options = ROTASTIC_GREETING_VIBES.slice();

  const message = options[Math.floor(Math.random() * options.length)];
  rotasticLastGreetingVibe = message;
  return message;
}

function buildRecurringDoctorGreeting() {
  const doctor = String(activeUserDocName || '').replace(/^Dr\.?\s*/i, '').trim();
  const first = doctor.split(/\s+/)[0] || 'Doctor';

  let hour = new Date().getHours();
  try {
    hour = parseInt(
      new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
        timeZone:'Asia/Qatar',
        hour:'2-digit',
        hour12:false
      }).format(new Date()),
      10
    );
  } catch (e) {}

  const greeting =
    hour < 12 ? 'Good morning' :
    hour < 17 ? 'Good afternoon' :
    'Good evening';

  const wish = nextGreetingVibe();
  return greeting + ', Dr ' + first + '.\n' + wish;
}

async function renderNextTickerSlot() {
  const ticker = document.getElementById('swapsNewsTicker');
  if (!ticker) return;

  if (rotasticActiveTab !== 'schedule') {
    ticker.classList.add('hidden');
    return;
  }

  if (!profileSetupIsComplete()) {
    renderPersistentFirstSetupTicker();
    return;
  }

  const launchNotification = rotasticTakeLaunchTickerNotification();
  if (launchNotification) {
    rotasticMarkNotificationShownOnce(launchNotification);
    const launchText = '🔔 ' + String(launchNotification.message || 'You have a new ROTASTIC notification.');
    renderTickerCard('notification', launchText, 15000);
    return;
  }

  // After all launch notifications have appeared once, preserve the standard
  // greeting → centre identity → normal announcement sequence.
  if (!rotasticOpeningSequenceHasRun && !rotasticOpeningTickerActive) {
    startOpeningTickerSequence();
    return;
  }

  const tickerFetches = await Promise.allSettled([
    fetchAbuNakhlaPrayerTimes(),
    refreshLiveClinicalUpdates(),
    refreshQatarAchievementNews(),
    refreshLocalTrafficUpdate(),
    refreshExternalHeadlineFeeds()
  ]);
  const prayerTimes = tickerFetches[0] && tickerFetches[0].status === 'fulfilled'
    ? tickerFetches[0].value
    : null;

  tickerSlotCounter += 1;

  const rawItem = String(Storage.get('rotastic_breaking_news_item', '') || '').trim();
  const meta = getActiveTickerMeta();

  // Live rota changes are highlighted in the same top ticker with a distinct style.
  const rotaAlert = Storage.getJSON('rotastic_rota_change_alert', null);
  if (rotaAlert && rotaAlert.message && Number(rotaAlert.expiresAt || 0) > Date.now()) {
    if (tickerSlotCounter % 3 === 1) {
      renderTickerCard('rota', rotaAlert.message, 8500);
      return;
    }
  }

  // In the final 30 minutes, insert a concise, positive personal message plus
  // tomorrow's rota note without stopping the existing ticke

async function renderNextTickerSlot() {
  const ticker = document.getElementById('swapsNewsTicker');
  if (!ticker) return;

  if (rotasticActiveTab !== 'schedule') {
    ticker.classList.add('hidden');
    return;
  }

  if (!profileSetupIsComplete()) {
    renderPersistentFirstSetupTicker();
    return;
  }

  const launchNotification = rotasticTakeLaunchTickerNotification();
  if (launchNotification) {
    rotasticMarkNotificationShownOnce(launchNotification);
    const launchText = '🔔 ' + String(launchNotification.message || 'You have a new ROTASTIC notification.');
    renderTickerCard('notification', launchText, 15000);
    return;
  }

  // After all launch notifications have appeared once, preserve the standard
  // greeting → centre identity → normal announcement sequence.
  if (!rotasticOpeningSequenceHasRun && !rotasticOpeningTickerActive) {
    startOpeningTickerSequence();
    return;
  }

  const tickerFetches = await Promise.allSettled([
    fetchAbuNakhlaPrayerTimes(),
    refreshLiveClinicalUpdates(),
    refreshQatarAchievementNews(),
    refreshLocalTrafficUpdate(),
    refreshExternalHeadlineFeeds()
  ]);
  const prayerTimes = tickerFetches[0] && tickerFetches[0].status === 'fulfilled'
    ? tickerFetches[0].value
    : null;

  tickerSlotCounter += 1;

  const rawItem = String(Storage.get('rotastic_breaking_news_item', '') || '').trim();
  const meta = getActiveTickerMeta();

  // Live rota changes are highlighted in the same top ticker with a distinct style.
  const rotaAlert = Storage.getJSON('rotastic_rota_change_alert', null);
  if (rotaAlert && rotaAlert.message && Number(rotaAlert.expiresAt || 0) > Date.now()) {
    if (tickerSlotCounter % 3 === 1) {
      renderTickerCard('rota', rotaAlert.message, 8500);
      return;
    }
  }

  // In the final 30 minutes, insert a concise, positive personal message plus
  // tomorrow's rota note without stopping the existing ticker/news rotation.
  const lastThirtyMessage = getLastThirtyMinuteTickerMessage();
  if (lastThirtyMessage && tickerSlotCounter % 3 === 1) {
    renderTickerCard('general', lastThirtyMessage, getTickerDisplayDuration(lastThirtyMessage, 7200));
    return;
  }
  const sickPriority = getSickAlertPriorityWindow(meta);
  const isSickPriorityActive = rawItem && meta.kind === 'sick' && sickPriority.active;
  const isAdminNotice = rawItem && meta.kind === 'admin';

  const prayerMoment = getPrayerMomentAlert(prayerTimes);
  if (prayerMoment && tickerSlotCounter % 2 === 1 && !isSickPriorityActive) {
    renderFullWidthTickerTakeover(
      'prayerMoment',
      prayerMoment.message,
      6500
    );
    return;
  }

  // Normal stream: fixed category order, shuffled content inside each category:
  // Every cycle includes motivational lines and practical clinical advice.
  // 0 Greeting (brief)
  // 1 Qatar / PHCC medical statistic
  // 2 Relevant prayer
  // 3 Qatar / Arab-region medical news
  // 4 Positive safe-practice advice
  // 5 Qatar / PHCC medical statistic
  // 6 Abu Nakhla identity animation
  // 7 Greeting (brief, different positive line)
  // 8 Qatar / Arab-region medical news
  // 9 Current QNA / Al Jazeera headline
  // 10 Qatar / PHCC medical statistic
  // 11 Positive doctor encouragement
  // 12 Qatar / Arab-region medical news
  // 13 NHS global/relevant health item
  // 14 Abu Nakhla identity animation
  // then repeat.
  // This makes public-health statistics and medical news much more visible.

  if (isAdminNotice && tickerSlotCounter % 3 === 1) {
    renderTickerCard('admin', conciseTickerText(rawItem, 105), 6500);
    return;
  }

  const cycleStep = rotasticContentCycleIndex % 15;
  rotasticContentCycleIndex = (rotasticContentCycleIndex + 1) % 15;

  if (cycleStep === 0 || cycleStep === 7) {
    renderTickerCard('encouragement', nextDoctorEncouragement(), 5800);
    return;
  }

  if (cycleStep === 1 || cycleStep === 5 || cycleStep === 10) {
    rotasticMedicalStatCycleCount += 1;

    // Keep the stream focused on public-health numbers. Qatar fun facts remain
    // occasional rather than replacing a quarter of the medical-stat slots.
    const stat = chooseWeightedMedicalStat();
    if (stat) {
      renderTickerCard('stat', stat, 6000);
      return;
    }

    renderTickerCard('encouragement', nextDoctorEncouragement(), 5600);
    return;
  }

  if (cycleStep === 2) {
    const prayerMessage = getPrayerTickerMessage(prayerTimes);
    if (prayerMessage) {
      renderTickerCard('prayer', conciseTickerText(prayerMessage, 98), 6000);
      return;
    }
    renderTickerCard('safepractice', nextSafePracticeAdvice(), 5600);
    return;
  }

  if (cycleStep === 3 || cycleStep === 8 || cycleStep === 12) {
    const medicalHeadline = chooseLatestMedicalHeadline();

    if (medicalHeadline) {
      renderTickerCard('medicalnews', medicalHeadline,
=== rotasticCurrentTickerLink @ 1627303 ===
',
    '📥 Clear one inbox item whenever there is a short gap.',
    '🩺 Chronic-disease reviews are easier with a checklist.',
    '👟 A quick foot check can catch problems early in diabetes.',
    '🫀 Ask about adherence before assuming treatment failure.',
    '📏 Correct cuff size matters for blood-pressure accuracy.',
    '🧂 Ask about salt, not just “healthy eating”.',
    '🧴 Check inhaler technique rather than assuming it is correct.',
    '📖 Teach-back can reveal misunderstandings quickly.',
    '👥 Teamwork is part of patient safety.',
    '🔒 Protect patient privacy even during rushed handovers.',
    '🌤️ A difficult hour does not mean a difficult whole shift.'
  ];

  const live = rotasticLiveClinicalUpdates.length
    ? rotasticLiveClinicalUpdates
    : ROTASTIC_CURATED_CLINICAL_UPDATES;

  return encouragement.concat(live);
}

function normalizeFeedTitle(value) {
  return String(value || '')
    .replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '')
    .replace(/&amp;/g, '&')
    .replace(/&#39;/g, "'")
    .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
    .trim();
}

async function fetchRssJson(url) {
  const response = await fetch(url, {headers:{'Accept':'application/json'}});
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Feed request failed');
  const payload = await response.json();
  return Array.isArray(payload.items) ? payload.items : [];
}

function regionalMedicalFeedUrl(query) {
  return 'https://api.rss2json.com/v1/api.json?rss_url=' +
    encodeURIComponent(
      'https://news.google.com/rss/search?q=' +
      encodeURIComponent(query) +
      '&hl=en&gl=QA&ceid=QA:en'
    );
}

function isBoringInstitutionalHealthHeadline(title) {
  const value = String(title || '').toLowerCase();
  return (
    /\b(publications?|homepage|portal|newsletter|annual report|media center|media centre)\b/i.test(value) ||
    /\b(latest|new)\s+(health|public health|primary care)?\s*updates?\b/i.test(value) ||
    /\bministry of public health\s*[-–:]?\s*publications?\b/i.test(value) ||
    /\bphcc\s*[-–:]?\s*(latest|updates?|publications?)\b/i.test(value)
  );
}

function isRelevantFamilyMedicineHeadline(title, sourceName) {
  const t=String(title||'').toLowerCase().trim(), s=String(sourceName||'').toLowerCase();
  if(!t||/publications?|newsletter|latest updates?|homepage|minister meets|conference to open/i.test(t)) return false;
  const topic=/(family medicine|primary care|general practice|randomized|trial|meta-analysis|guideline|glp-1|semaglutide|tirzepatide|obesity|diabetes|hypertension|cardiovascular|screening|vaccine|measles|influenza|antibiotic|antimicrobial|mental health|cancer|microbiome|dementia|asthma|copd|smoking|prevention)/i;
  const trusted=/(jama|nejm|lancet|bmj|nature|annals|medscape|who|cdc|fda|sidra|hamad)/i;
  return topic.test(t)&&(trusted.test(t)||trusted.test(s));
}

function cleanInterestingMedicalTickerText(title, sourceName) {
  return cleanRegionalMedicalHeadline(title, sourceName);
}

async function fetchRegionalMedicalSource(source) {
  try {
    const items = await fetchRssJson(regionalMedicalFeedUrl(source.query));

    return items
      .map(function(item) {
        return {
          title: normalizeFeedTitle(item.title),
          link: String(item.link || item.url || source.home || ''),
          sourceName: source.name,
          domain: source.domain,
          priority: source.priority || 1
        };
      })
      .filter(function(item) {
        return item.title &&
          item.title.length > 18 &&
          isRelevantFamilyMedicineHeadline(item.title, item.sourceName);
      })
      .map(function(item) {
        item.title = cleanInterestingMedicalTickerText(item.title, item.sourceName);
        return item;
      })
      .slice(0,6);
  } catch (e) {
    return [];
  }
}

function cleanRegionalMedicalHeadline(title, sourceName) {
  let value = String(title || '')
    .replace(/\s+-\s+Google News\s*$/i, '')
    .replace(/\s+-\s+[^-]{2,45}$/i, function(match) {
      const source = String(sourceName || '').toLowerCase();
      const tail = match.toLowerCase();
      return tail.includes(source.split(' ')[0]) ? '' : match;
    })
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim();
  return fitNormalTickerCopy(value, 15, 108);
}


async function fetchRotasticNewsProxy(kind) {
  try {
    const response = await fetch(
      ROTASTIC_NEWS_PROXY_ENDPOINT + '?kind=' + encodeURIComponent(kind || 'medical'),
      {headers:{'Accept':'application/json'}}
    );
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error('ROTASTIC news proxy request failed');
    const payload = await response.json();
    return Array.isArray(payload.items) ? payload.items : [];
  } catch (e) {
    return [];
  }
}

function chooseNiceGuidanceTickerItem() {
  const item = ROTASTIC_NICE_GUIDANCE_UPDATES[Math.floor(Math.random() * ROTASTIC_NICE_GUIDANCE_UPDATES.length)];
  if (!item) return '';
  rotasticCurrentMedicalSourceMeta = {name:'NICE', domain:'nice.org.uk'};
  rotasticCurrentMedicalLink = item.link || 'https://www.nice.org.uk/';
  return makeShortMedicalHeadline(item.title, 'NICE') || '';
}

function getActualDohaWeatherTickerText() {
  const weather = readCachedDohaWeather();
  if (!weather || typeof weather.temp !== 'number') return '';
  const label = String(weather.label || 'current conditions').toLowerCase();
  return 'Doha now: ' + Math.round(weather.temp) + '°C, ' + label + '.';
}

function makeShortNewsHeadline(title) {
  const raw = String(title || '')
    .replace(/\s+-\s+(Reuters|Al Jazeera|Qatar News Agency|QNA|Al Arabiya)\s*$/i, '')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim();

  // Show the actual headline only when it can fit cleanly. Never replace it
  // with vague text such as “regional weather update”.
  return fitNormalTickerCopy(raw, 15, 108);
}

function chooseAlJazeeraHeadline() {
  let pool = rotasticAlJazeeraHeadlines;
  if (!pool.length) pool = Storage.getJSON('rotastic_cached_aljazeera_headlines', []) || [];
  if (!pool.length) return '';
  const item = pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * Math.min(pool.length, 10))];
  const title = typeof item === 'string' ? item : item.title;
  const link = typeof item === 'string' ? '' : (item.link || '');
  const shortTitle = makeShortNewsHeadline(title);
  if (!shortTitle) return '';
  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = link;
  return shortTitle;
}



const ROTASTIC_NHS_RELEVANT_TIPS = [];

function chooseNhsRelevantHeadline() {
  let pool = rotasticNhsHeadlines;
  if (!pool.length) pool = Storage.getJSON('rotastic_cached_nhs_headlines', []) || [];
  if (!pool.length) return '';
  const shuffled = pool.slice().sort(function(){ return Math.random() - .5; });
  for (const item of shuffled.slice(0,10)) {
    const title = typeof item === 'string' ? item : item.title;
    const link = typeof item === 'string' ? '' : (item.link || '');
    const shortTitle = makeShortMedicalHeadline(String(title || '').replace(/\s*-\s*NHS.*$/i, ''), 'NHS');
    if (!shortTitle) continue;
    rotasticCurrentMedicalSourceMeta = {name:'NHS', domain:'nhs.uk'};
    rotasticCurrentMedicalLink = link;
    return shortTitle;
  }
  return '';
}

function chooseWorldHeadlineWithSource() {
  const picks = [];

  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = '';
  const qna = chooseQnaHeadline();
  if (qna) picks.push({type:'qnanews', text:qna, source:{name:'Qatar News Agency',domain:'qna.org.qa'}, link:rotasticCurrentTickerLink});

  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = '';
  const aj = chooseAlJazeeraHeadline();
  if (aj) picks.push({type:'worldnews', text:aj, source:{name:'Al Jazeera English',domain:'aljazeera.com'}, link:rotasticCurrentTickerLink});

  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = '';
  const reuters = chooseReutersHeadline();
  if (reuters) picks.push({type:'reutersnews', text:reuters, source:{name:'Reuters',domain:'reuters.com'}, link:rotasticCurrentTickerLink});

  if (!picks.length) return null;
  let available = picks.filter(function(p){ return !rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines.includes(p.text); });
  if (!available.length) available = picks;
  const chosen = available[rotasticWorldHeadlineSourceIndex % available.length];
  rotasticWorldHeadlineSourceIndex++;
  rotasticCurrentTickerSourceMeta = chosen.source;
  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = chosen.link || '';
  rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines.push(chosen.text);
  if (rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines.length > 6) rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines.shift();
  return {type:chosen.type, text:chosen.text};
}

let rotasticSafeAdviceIndex = -1;
let rotasticEncouragementIndex = -1;
let rotasticSafePracticeBag = [];
let rotasticEncouragementBag = [];
let rotasticMedicalStatBag = [];
let rotasticRecentMedicalHeadlines = [];
let rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines = [];

function rotasticRefillShuffleBag(length, lastIndex) {
  const bag = Array.from({length:length}, function(_, i){ return i; });
  for (let i = bag.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    const tmp = bag[i]; bag[i] = bag[j]; bag[j] = tmp;
  }
  if (bag.length > 1 && bag[0] === lastIndex) {
    const tmp = bag[0]; bag[0] = bag[1]; bag[1] = tmp;
  }
  return bag;
}

function nextSafePracticeAdvice() {
  if (!ROTASTIC_SAFE_PRACTICE_ADVICE.length) return '';
  if (!rotasticSafePracticeBag.length) {
    rotasticSafePracticeBag = rotasticRefillShuffleBag(
      ROTASTIC_SAFE_PRACTICE_ADVICE.length,
      rotasticSafeAdviceIndex
    );
  }
  const index = rotasticSafePracticeBag.shift();
  rotasticSafeAdviceIndex = index;
  return ROTASTIC_SAFE_PRACTICE_ADVICE[index];
}

  '🤲 Relieving another person’s distress is a deed of great reward. — Sahih Muslim 2699'
];

let rotasticSafeAdviceIndex = -1;
let rotasticEncouragementIndex = -1;
let rotasticSafePracticeBag = [];
let rotasticEncouragementBag = [];
let rotasticMedicalStatBag = [];
let rotasticRecentMedicalHeadlines = [];
let rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines = [];

function rotasticRefillShuffleBag(length, lastIndex) {
  const bag = Array.from({length:length}, function(_, i){ return i; });
  for (let i = bag.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    const tmp = bag[i]; bag[i] = bag[j]; bag[j] = tmp;
  }
  if (bag.length > 1 && bag[0] === lastIndex) {
    const tmp = bag[0]; bag[0] = bag[1]; bag[1] = tmp;
  }
  return bag;
}

function nextSafePracticeAdvice() {
  if (!ROTASTIC_SAFE_PRACTICE_ADVICE.length) return '';
  if (!rotasticSafePracticeBag.length) {
    rotasticSafePracticeBag = rotasticRefillShuffleBag(
      ROTASTIC_SAFE_PRACTICE_ADVICE.length,
      rotasticSafeAdviceIndex
    );
  }
  const index = rotasticSafePracticeBag.shift();
  rotasticSafeAdviceIndex = index;
  return ROTASTIC_SAFE_PRACTICE_ADVICE[index];
}


function nextDoctorEncouragement() {
  if (!ROTASTIC_DOCTOR_ENCOURAGEMENT.length) return '';
  if (!rotasticEncouragementBag.length) {
    rotasticEncouragementBag = rotasticRefillShuffleBag(
      ROTASTIC_DOCTOR_ENCOURAGEMENT.length,
      rotasticEncouragementIndex
    );
  }
  const index = rotasticEncouragementBag.shift();
  rotasticEncouragementIndex = index;
  return ROTASTIC_DOCTOR_ENCOURAGEMENT[index];
}

function fitNormalTickerCopy(value, maxWords, maxChars) {
  const text = String(value || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  if (!text || text.includes('…') || text.includes('...')) return '';
  const words = text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (words.length > Number(maxWords || 16)) return '';
  if (text.length > Number(maxChars || 112)) return '';
  return text;
}

function getTickerCopySizeClass(message) {
  const text = String(message || '').trim();
  const words = text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;
  if (words <= 8 && text.length <= 58) return 'ticker-copy-short';
  if (words <= 12 && text.length <= 86) return 'ticker-copy-medium';
  return 'ticker-copy-long';
}

function conciseTickerText(value, maxChars) {
  const text = String(value || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  const max = Math.max(58, Number(maxChars || 92));
  if (text.length <= max) return text;
  return '';
}

function getPrayerTickerMessage(prayerTimes) {
  return choosePrayerMessa

    const shortTitle = makeShortMedicalHeadline(String(title || '').replace(/\s*-\s*NHS.*$/i, ''), 'NHS');
    if (!shortTitle) continue;
    rotasticCurrentMedicalSourceMeta = {name:'NHS', domain:'nhs.uk'};
    rotasticCurrentMedicalLink = link;
    return shortTitle;
  }
  return '';
}

function chooseQnaHeadline() {
  let pool = rotasticQnaHeadlines;
  if (!pool.length) pool = Storage.getJSON('rotastic_cached_qna_headlines', []) || [];
  if (!pool.length) return '';
  const item = pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * Math.min(pool.length, 10))];
  const title = typeof item === 'string' ? item : item.title;
  const link = typeof item === 'string' ? '' : (item.link || '');
  const shortTitle = makeShortNewsHeadline(String(title || '').replace(/\s*-\s*Qatar News Agency\s*$/i, ''));
  if (!shortTitle) return '';
  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = link;
  return shortTitle;
}

function chooseAlArabiyaHeadline(){let pool=rotasticAlArabiyaHeadlines;if(!pool.length)pool=Storage.getJSON('rotastic_cached_alarabiya_headlines',[])||[];if(!pool.length)return '';const item=pool[Math.floor(Math.random()*Math.min(pool.length,8))];const title=typeof item==='string'?item:item.title;rotasticCurrentTickerLink=typeof item==='string'?'':(item.link||'');rotasticCurrentTickerSourceMeta={name:'Al Arabiya',domain:'english.alarabiya.net'};const shortTitle=makeShortNewsHeadline(String(title||''));return shortTitle||'';}

function chooseReutersHeadline() {
  let pool = rotasticReutersHeadlines;
  if (!pool.length) pool = Storage.getJSON('rotastic_cached_reuters_headlines', []) || [];
  if (!pool.length) return '';
  const item = pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * Math.min(pool.length, 10))];
  const title = typeof item === 'string' ? item : item.title;
  const link = typeof item === 'string' ? '' : (item.link || '');
  const shortTitle = makeShortNewsHeadline(title);
  if (!shortTitle) return '';
  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = link;
  return shortTitle;
}

function chooseWorldHeadlineWithSource() {
  const picks = [];

  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = '';
  const qna = chooseQnaHeadline();
  if (qna) picks.push({type:'qnanews', text:qna, source:{name:'Qatar News Agency',domain:'qna.org.qa'}, link:rotasticCurrentTickerLink});

  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = '';
  const aj = chooseAlJazeeraHeadline();
  if (aj) picks.push({type:'worldnews', text:aj, source:{name:'Al Jazeera English',domain:'aljazeera.com'}, link:rotasticCurrentTickerLink});

  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = '';
  const reuters = chooseReutersHeadline();
  if (reuters) picks.push({type:'reutersnews', text:reuters, source:{name:'Reuters',domain:'reuters.com'}, link:rotasticCurrentTickerLink});

  if (!picks.length) return null;
  let available = picks.filter(function(p){ return !rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines.includes(p.text); });
  if (!available.length) available = picks;
  const chosen = available[rotasticWorldHeadlineSourceIndex % available.length];
  rotasticWorldHeadlineSourceIndex++;
  rotasticCurrentTickerSourceMeta = chosen.source;
  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = chosen.link || '';
  rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines.push(chosen.text);
  if (rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines.length > 6) rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines.shift();
  return {type:chosen.type, text:chosen.text};
}

  '🌙 Fatigue increases errors — slow down when the shift gets long.'
];

  '🌟 You are a good doctor. Keep going.',
  '💙 Your calm care makes a difference.',
  '🩺 Trust your training and your judgement.',
  '✨ One good consultation can change a day.',

let rotasticSafeAdviceIndex = -1;
let rotasticEncouragementIndex = -1;
let rotasticSafePracticeBag = [];
let rotasticEncouragementBag = [];
let rotasticMedicalStatBag = [];
let rotasticRecentMedicalHeadlines = [];
let rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines = [];

function rotasticRefillShuffleBag(length, lastIndex) {
  const bag = Array.from({length:length}, function(_, i){ return i; });
  for (let i = bag.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    const tmp = bag[i]; bag[i] = bag[j]; bag[j] = tmp;
  }
  if (bag.length > 1 && bag[0] === lastIndex) {
    const tmp = bag[0]; bag[0] = bag[1]; bag[1] = tmp;
  }
  return bag;
}

function nextSafePracticeAdvice() {
  if (!ROTASTIC_SAFE_PRACTICE_ADVICE.length) return '';
  if (!rotasticSafePracticeBag.length) {
    rotasticSafePracticeBag = rotasticRefillShuffleBag(
      ROTASTIC_SAFE_PRACTICE_ADVICE.length,
      rotasticSafeAdviceIndex
    );
  }
  const index = rotasticSafePracticeBag.shift();
  rotasticSafeAdviceIndex = index;
  return ROTASTIC_SAFE_PRACTICE_ADVICE[index];
}


function nextDoctorEncouragement() {
  if (!ROTASTIC_DOCTOR_ENCOURAGEMENT.length) return '';
  if (!rotasticEncouragementBag.length) {
    rotasticEncouragementBag = rotasticRefillShuffleBag(
      ROTASTIC_DOCTOR_ENCOURAGEMENT.length,
      rotasticEncouragementIndex
    );
  }
  const index = rotasticEncouragementBag.shift();
  rotasticEncouragementIndex = index;
  return ROTASTIC_DOCTOR_ENCOURAGEMENT[index];
}

function fitNormalTickerCopy(value, maxWords, maxChars) {
  const text = String(value || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  if (!text || text.includes('…') || text.includes('...')) return '';
  const words = text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (words.length > Number(maxWords || 16)) return '';
  if (text.length > Number(maxChars || 112)) return '';
  return text;
}

function getTickerCopySizeClass(message) {
  const text = String(message || '').trim();
  const words = text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;
  if (words <= 8 && text.length <= 58) return 'ticker-copy-short';
  if (words <= 12 && text.length <= 86) return 'ticker-copy-medium';
  return 'ticker-copy-long';
}

function conciseTickerText(value, maxChars) {
  const text = String(value || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  const max = Math.max(58, Number(maxChars || 92));
  if (text.length <= max) return text;
  return '';
}

function getPrayerTickerMessage(prayerTimes) {
  return choosePrayerMessage(prayerTimes) || '';
}

function chooseReutersHeadline() {
  let pool = rotasticReutersHeadlines;
  if (!pool.length) pool = Storage.getJSON('rotastic_cached_reuters_headlines', []) || [];
  if (!pool.length) return '';
  const item = pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * Math.min(pool.length, 10))];
  const title = typeof item === 'string' ? item : item.title;
  const link = typeof item === 'string' ? '' : (item.link || '');
  const shortTitle = makeShortNewsHeadline(title);
  if (!shortTitle) return '';
  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = link;
  return shortTitle;
}

function chooseWorldHeadlineWithSource() {
  const picks = [];

  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = '';
  const qna = chooseQnaHeadline();
  if (qna) picks.push({type:'qnanews', text:qna, source:{name:'Qatar News Agency',domain:'qna.org.qa'}, link:rotasticCurrentTickerLink});

  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = '';
  const aj = chooseAlJazeeraHeadline();
  if (aj) picks.push({type:'worldnews', text:aj, source:{name:'Al Jazeera English',domain:'aljazeera.com'}, link:rotasticCurrentTickerLink});

  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = '';
  const reuters = chooseReutersHeadline();
  if (reuters) picks.push({type:'reutersnews', text:reuters, source:{name:'Reuters',domain:'reuters.com'}, link:rotasticCurrentTickerLink});

  if (!picks.length) return null;
  let available = picks.filter(function(p){ return !rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines.includes(p.text); });
  if (!available.length) available = picks;
  const chosen = available[rotasticWorldHeadlineSourceIndex % available.length];
  rotasticWorldHeadlineSourceIndex++;
  rotasticCurrentTickerSourceMeta = chosen.source;
  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = chosen.link || '';
  rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines.push(chosen.text);
  if (rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines.length > 6) rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines.shift();
  return {type:chosen.type, text:chosen.text};
}

let rotasticSafeAdviceIndex = -1;
let rotasticEncouragementIndex = -1;
let rotasticSafePracticeBag = [];
let rotasticEncouragementBag = [];
let rotasticMedicalStatBag = [];
let rotasticRecentMedicalHeadlines = [];
let rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines = [];

function rotasticRefillShuffleBag(length, lastIndex) {
  const bag = Array.from({length:length}, function(_, i){ return i; });
  for (let i = bag.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    const tmp = bag[i]; bag[i] = bag[j]; bag[j] = tmp;
  }
  if (bag.length > 1 && bag[0] === lastIndex) {
    const tmp = bag[0]; bag[0] = bag[1]; bag[1] = tmp;
  }
  return bag;
}

function nextSafePracticeAdvice() {
  if (!ROTASTIC_SAFE_PRACTICE_ADVICE.length) return '';
  if (!rotasticSafePracticeBag.length) {
    rotasticSafePracticeBag = rotasticRefillShuffleBag(
      ROTASTIC_SAFE_PRACTICE_ADVICE.length,
      rotasticSafeAdviceIndex
    );
  }
  const index = rotasticSafePracticeBag.shift();
  rotasticSafeAdviceIndex = index;
  return ROTASTIC_SAFE_PRACTICE_ADVICE[index];
}


function nextDoctorEncouragement() {
  if (!ROTASTIC_DOCTOR_ENCOURAGEMENT.length) return '';
  if (!rotasticEncouragementBag.length) {
    rotasticEncouragementBag = rotasticRefillShuffleBag(
      ROTASTIC_DOCTOR_ENCOURAGEMENT.length,
      rotasticEncouragementIndex
    );
  }
  const index = rotasticEncouragementBag.shift();
  rotasticEncouragementIndex = index;
  return ROTASTIC_DOCTOR_ENCOURAGEMENT[index];
}

function fitNormalTickerCopy(value, maxWords, maxChars) {
  const text = String(value || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  if (!text || text.includes('…') || text.includes('...')) return '';
  const words = text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (words.length > Number(maxWords || 16)) return '';
  if (text.length > Number(maxChars || 112)) return '';
  return text;
}

function getTickerCopySizeClass(message) {
  const text = String(message || '').trim();
  const words = text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;
  if (words <= 8 && text.length <= 58) return 'ticker-copy-short';
  if (words <= 12 && text.length <= 86) return 'ticker-copy-medium';
  return 'ticker-copy-long';
}

function conciseTickerText(value, maxChars) {
  const text = String(value || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  const max = Math.max(58, Number(maxChars || 92));
  if (text.length <= max) return text;
  return '';
}

function getPrayerTickerMessage(prayerTimes) {
  return choosePrayerMessage(prayerTimes) || '';
}

function chooseReutersHeadline() {
  let pool = rotasticReutersHeadlines;
  if (!pool.length) pool = Storage.getJSON('rotastic_cached_reuters_headlines', []) || [];
  if (!pool.length) return '';
  const item = pool[Math.floor(Math.random() * Math.min(pool.length, 10))];
  const title = typeof item === 'string' ? item : item.title;
  const link = typeof item === 'string' ? '' : (item.link || '');
  const shortTitle = makeShortNewsHeadline(title);
  if (!shortTitle) return '';
  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = link;
  return shortTitle;
}

function chooseWorldHeadlineWithSource() {
  const picks = [];

  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = '';
  const qna = chooseQnaHeadline();
  if (qna) picks.push({type:'qnanews', text:qna, source:{name:'Qatar News Agency',domain:'qna.org.qa'}, link:rotasticCurrentTickerLink});

  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = '';
  const aj = chooseAlJazeeraHeadline();
  if (aj) picks.push({type:'worldnews', text:aj, source:{name:'Al Jazeera English',domain:'aljazeera.com'}, link:rotasticCurrentTickerLink});

  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = '';
  const reuters = chooseReutersHeadline();
  if (reuters) picks.push({type:'reutersnews', text:reuters, source:{name:'Reuters',domain:'reuters.com'}, link:rotasticCurrentTickerLink});

  if (!picks.length) return null;
  let available = picks.filter(function(p){ return !rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines.includes(p.text); });
  if (!available.length) available = picks;
  const chosen = available[rotasticWorldHeadlineSourceIndex % available.length];
  rotasticWorldHeadlineSourceIndex++;
  rotasticCurrentTickerSourceMeta = chosen.source;
  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = chosen.link || '';
  rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines.push(chosen.text);
  if (rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines.length > 6) rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines.shift();
  return {type:chosen.type, text:chosen.text};
}

}

function chooseWorldHeadlineWithSource() {
  const picks = [];

  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = '';
  const qna = chooseQnaHeadline();
  if (qna) picks.push({type:'qnanews', text:qna, source:{name:'Qatar News Agency',domain:'qna.org.qa'}, link:rotasticCurrentTickerLink});

  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = '';
  const aj = chooseAlJazeeraHeadline();
  if (aj) picks.push({type:'worldnews', text:aj, source:{name:'Al Jazeera English',domain:'aljazeera.com'}, link:rotasticCurrentTickerLink});

  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = '';
  const reuters = chooseReutersHeadline();
  if (reuters) picks.push({type:'reutersnews', text:reuters, source:{name:'Reuters',domain:'reuters.com'}, link:rotasticCurrentTickerLink});

  if (!picks.length) return null;
  let available = picks.filter(function(p){ return !rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines.includes(p.text); });
  if (!available.length) available = picks;
  const chosen = available[rotasticWorldHeadlineSourceIndex % available.length];
  rotasticWorldHeadlineSourceIndex++;
  rotasticCurrentTickerSourceMeta = chosen.source;
  rotasticCurrentTickerLink = chosen.link || '';
  rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines.push(chosen.text);
  if (rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines.length > 6) rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines.shift();
  return {type:chosen.type, text:chosen.text};
}

let rotasticSafeAdviceIndex = -1;
let rotasticEncouragementIndex = -1;
let rotasticSafePracticeBag = [];
let rotasticEncouragementBag = [];
let rotasticMedicalStatBag = [];
let rotasticRecentMedicalHeadlines = [];
let rotasticRecentWorldHeadlines = [];

function rotasticRefillShuffleBag(length, lastIndex) {
  const bag = Array.from({length:length}, function(_, i){ return i; });
  for (let i = bag.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    const tmp = bag[i]; bag[i] = bag[j]; bag[j] = tmp;
  }
  if (bag.length > 1 && bag[0] === lastIndex) {
    const tmp = bag[0]; bag[0] = bag[1]; bag[1] = tmp;
  }
  return bag;
}

function nextSafePracticeAdvice() {
  if (!ROTASTIC_SAFE_PRACTICE_ADVICE.length) return '';
  if (!rotasticSafePracticeBag.length) {
    rotasticSafePracticeBag = rotasticRefillShuffleBag(
      ROTASTIC_SAFE_PRACTICE_ADVICE.length,
      rotasticSafeAdviceIndex
    );
  }
  const index = rotasticSafePracticeBag.shift();
  rotasticSafeAdviceIndex = index;
  return ROTASTIC_SAFE_PRACTICE_ADVICE[index];
}


function nextDoctorEncouragement() {
  if (!ROTASTIC_DOCTOR_ENCOURAGEMENT.length) return '';
  if (!rotasticEncouragementBag.length) {
    rotasticEncouragementBag = rotasticRefillShuffleBag(
      ROTASTIC_DOCTOR_ENCOURAGEMENT.length,
      rotasticEncouragementIndex
    );
  }
  const index = rotasticEncouragementBag.shift();
  rotasticEncouragementIndex = index;
  return ROTASTIC_DOCTOR_ENCOURAGEMENT[index];
}

function fitNormalTickerCopy(value, maxWords, maxChars) {
  const text = String(value || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  if (!text || text.includes('…') || text.includes('...')) return '';
  const words = text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (words.length > Number(maxWords || 16)) return '';
  if (text.length > Number(maxChars || 112)) return '';
  return text;
}

function getTickerCopySizeClass(message) {
  const text = String(message || '').trim();
  const words = text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;
  if (words <= 8 && text.length <= 58) return 'ticker-copy-short';
  if (words <= 12 && text.length <= 86) return 'ticker-copy-medium';
  return 'ticker-copy-long';
}

function conciseTickerText(value, maxChars) {
  const text = String(value || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  const max = Math.max(58, Number(maxChars || 92));
  if (text.length <= max) return text;
  return '';
}

function getPrayerTickerMessage(prayerTimes) {
  return choosePrayerMessage(prayerTimes) || '';
}

function choosePrayerMessage(prayerTimes) {
  const list = getRelevantPrayerMessages(prayerTimes);
  if (!list.length) return '';
  const message = list[prayerTickerIndex % list.length];
  prayerTickerIndex = (prayerTickerIndex + 1) % list.length;
  return message;
}

let rotasticLastGreetingVibe = '';

function nextGreetingVibe() {
  if (!ROTASTIC_GREETING_VIBES.length) return '';

  let options = ROTASTIC_GREETING_VIBES.filter(function(msg) {
    return msg !== rotasticLastGreetingVibe;
  });

  if (!options.length) options = ROTASTIC_GREETING_VIBES.slice();

  const message = options[Math.floor(Math.random() * options.length)];
  rotasticLastGreetingVibe = message;
  return message;
}

function buildRecurringDoctorGreeting() {
  const doctor = String(activeUserDocName || '').replace(/^Dr\.?\s*/i, '').trim();
  const first = doctor.split(/\s+/)[0] || 'Doctor';

  let hour = new Date().getHours();
  try {
    hour = parseInt(
      new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
        timeZone:'Asia/Qatar',
        hour:'2-digit',
        hour12:false
      }).format(new Date()),
      10
    );
  } catch (e) {}

  const greeting =
    hour < 12 ? 'Good morning' :
    hour < 17 ? 'Good afternoon' :
    'Good evening';

  const wish = nextGreetingVibe();
  return greeting + ', Dr ' + first + '.\n' + wish;
}

async function renderNextTickerSlot() {
  const ticker = document.getElementById('swapsNewsTicker');
  if (!ticker) return;

  if (rotasticActiveTab !== 'schedule') {
    ticker.classList.add('hidden');
    return;
  }

  if (!profileSetupIsComplete()) {
    renderPersistentFirstSetupTicker();
    return;
  }

  const launchNotification = rotasticTakeLaunchTickerNotification();
  if (launchNotification) {
    rotasticMarkNotificationShownOnce(launchNotification);
    const launchText = '🔔 ' + String(launchNotification.message || 'You have a new ROTASTIC notification.');
    renderTickerCard('notification', launchText, 15000);
    return;
  }

  // After all launch notifications have appeared once, preserve the standard
  // greeting → centre identity → normal announcement sequence.
  if (!rotasticOpeningSequenceHasRun && !rotasticOpeningTickerActive) {
    startOpeningTickerSequence();
    return;
  }

  const tickerFetches = await Promise.allSettled([
    fetchAbuNakhlaPrayerTimes(),
    refreshLiveClinicalUpdates(),
    refreshQatarAchievementNews(),
    refreshLocalTrafficUpdate(),
    refreshExternalHeadlineFeeds()
  ]);
  const prayerTimes = tickerFetches[0] && tickerFetches[0].status === 'fulfilled'
    ? tickerFetches[0].value
    : null;

  tickerSlotCounter += 1;

  const rawItem = String(Storage.get('rotastic_breaking_news_item', '') || '').trim();
  const meta = getActiveTickerMeta();

  // Live rota changes are highlighted in the same top ticker with a distinct style.
  const rotaAlert = Storage.getJSON('rotastic_rota_change_alert', null);
  if (rotaAlert && rotaAlert.message && Number(rotaAlert.expiresAt || 0) > Date.now()) {
    if (tickerSlotCounter % 3 === 1) {
      renderTickerCard('rota', rotaAlert.message, 8500);
      return;
    }
  }

  // In the final 30 minutes, insert a concise, positive personal message plus
  // tomorrow's rota note without stopping the existing ticker/news rotation.
  const lastThirtyMessage = getLastThirtyMinuteTickerMessage();
  if (lastThirtyMessage && tickerSlotCounter % 3 === 1) {
    renderTickerCard('general', lastThirtyMessage, getTickerDisplayDuration(lastThirtyMessage, 7200));
    return;
  }
  const sickPriority = getSickAlertPriorityWindow(meta);
  const isSickPriorityActive = rawItem && meta.kind === 'sick' && sickPriority.active;
  const isAdminNotice = rawItem && meta.kind === 'admin';

  const prayerMoment = getPrayerMomentAlert(prayerTimes);
  if (prayerMoment && tickerSlotCounter % 2 === 1 && !isSickPriorityActive) {
    renderFullWidthTickerTakeover(
      'prayerMoment',
      prayerMoment.message,
      6500
    );
    return;
  }

  // Normal stream: fixed category order, shuffled content inside each category:
  // Every cycle includes motivational lines and practical clinical advice.
  // 0 Greeting (brief)
  // 1 Qatar / PHCC medical statistic
  // 2 Relevant prayer
  // 3 Qatar / Arab-region medical news
  // 4 Positive safe-practice advice
  // 5 Qatar / PHCC medical statistic
  // 6 Abu Nakhla identity animation
  // 7 Greeting (brief, different positive line)
  // 8 Qatar / Arab-region medical news
  // 9 Current QNA / Al Jazeera headline
  // 10 Qatar / PHCC medical statistic
  // 11 Positive doctor encouragement
  // 12 Qatar / Arab-region medical news
  // 13 NHS global/relevant health item
  // 14 Abu Nakhla identity animation
  // then repeat.
  // This makes public-health statistics and medical news much more visible.

  if (isAdminNotice && tickerSlotCounter % 3 === 1) {
    renderTickerCard('admin', conciseTickerText(rawItem, 105), 6500);
    return;
  }

  const cycleStep = rotasticContentCycleIndex % 15;
  rotasticContentCycleIndex = (rotasticContentCycleIndex + 1) % 15;

  if (cycleStep === 0 || cycleStep === 7) {
    renderTickerCard('encouragement', nextDoctorEncouragement(), 5800);
    return;
  }

  if (cycleStep === 1 || cycleStep === 5 || cycleStep === 10) {
    rotasticMedicalStatCycleCount += 1;

    // Keep the stream focused on p

function renderTickerCard(type, message, durationMs, fromHistory) {
  const immediate = type === 'sick' || type === 'greeting' || type === 'welcome';
  rotasticRunTickerTransition(function() {
    renderTickerCardNow(type, message, durationMs, fromHistory);
  }, immediate);
}

function choosePrayerMessage(prayerTimes) {
  const list = getRelevantPrayerMessages(prayerTimes);
  if (!list.length) return '';
  const message = list[prayerTickerIndex % list.length];
  prayerTickerIndex = (prayerTickerIndex + 1) % list.length;
  return message;
}

  'Hope the day runs gently from start to finish.',
  'Wishing you good energy for the whole shift.',
  'Have a safe shift and be kind to yourself too.'
];

let rotasticLastGreetingVibe = '';

function nextGreetingVibe() {
  if (!ROTASTIC_GREETING_VIBES.length) return '';

  let options = ROTASTIC_GREETING_VIBES.filter(function(msg) {
    return msg !== rotasticLastGreetingVibe;
  });

  if (!options.length) options = ROTASTIC_GREETING_VIBES.slice();

  const message = options[Math.floor(Math.random() * options.length)];
  rotasticLastGreetingVibe = message;
  return message;
}

function buildRecurringDoctorGreeting() {
  const doctor = String(activeUserDocName || '').replace(/^Dr\.?\s*/i, '').trim();
  const first = doctor.split(/\s+/)[0] || 'Doctor';

  let hour = new Date().getHours();
  try {
    hour = parseInt(
      new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
        timeZone:'Asia/Qatar',
        hour:'2-digit',
        hour12:false
      }).format(new Date()),
      10
    );
  } catch (e) {}

  const greeting =
    hour < 12 ? 'Good morning' :
    hour < 17 ? 'Good afternoon' :
    'Good evening';

  const wish = nextGreetingVibe();
  return greeting + ', Dr ' + first + '.\n' + wish;
}

async function renderNextTickerSlot() {
  const ticker = document.getElementById('swapsNewsTicker');
  if (!ticker) return;

  if (rotasticActiveTab !== 'schedule') {
    ticker.classList.add('hidden');
    return;
  }

  if (!profileSetupIsComplete()) {
    renderPersistentFirstSetupTicker();
    return;
  }

  const launchNotification = rotasticTakeLaunchTickerNotification();
  if (launchNotification) {
    rotasticMarkNotificationShownOnce(launchNotification);
    const launchText = '🔔 ' + String(launchNotification.message || 'You have a new ROTASTIC notification.');
    renderTickerCard('notification', launchText, 15000);
    return;
  }

  // After all launch notifications have appeared once, preserve the standard
  // greeting → centre identity → normal announcement sequence.
  if (!rotasticOpeningSequenceHasRun && !rotasticOpeningTickerActive) {
    startOpeningTickerSequence();
    return;
  }

  const tickerFetches = await Promise.allSettled([
    fetchAbuNakhlaPrayerTimes(),
    refreshLiveClinicalUpdates(),
    refreshQatarAchievementNews(),
    refreshLocalTrafficUpdate(),
    refreshExternalHeadlineFeeds()
  ]);
  const prayerTimes = tickerFetches[0] && tickerFetches[0].status === 'fulfilled'
    ? tickerFetches[0].value
    : null;

  tickerSlotCounter += 1;

  const rawItem = String(Storage.get('rotastic_breaking_news_item', '') || '').trim();
  const meta = getActiveTickerMeta();

  // Live rota changes are highlighted in the same top ticker with a distinct style.
  const rotaAlert = Storage.getJSON('rotastic_rota_change_alert', null);
  if (rotaAlert && rotaAlert.message && Number(rotaAlert.expiresAt || 0) > Date.now()) {
    if (tickerSlotCounter % 3 === 1) {
      renderTickerCard('rota', rotaAlert.message, 8500);
      return;
    }
  }

  // In the final 30 minutes, insert a concise, positive personal message plus
  // tomorrow's rota note without stopping the existing ticker/news rotation.
  const lastThirtyMessage = getLastThirtyMinuteTickerMessage();
  if (lastThirtyMessage && tickerSlotCounter % 3 === 1) {
    renderTickerCard('general', lastThirtyMessage, getTickerDisplayDuration(lastThirtyMessage, 7200));
    return;
  }
  const sickPriority = getSickAlertPriorityWindow(meta);
  const isSickPriorityActive = rawItem && meta.kind === 'sick' && sickPriority.active;
  const isAdminNotice = rawItem && meta.kind === 'admin';

  const prayerMoment = getPrayerMomentAlert(prayerTimes);
  if (prayerMoment && tickerSlotCounter % 2 === 1 && !isSickPriorityActive) {
    renderFullWidthTickerTakeover(
      'prayerMoment',
      prayerMoment.message,
      6500
    );
    return;
  }

  // Normal stream: fixed category order, shuffled content inside each category:
  // Every cycle includes motivational lines and practical clinical advice.
  // 0 Greeting (brief)
  // 1 Qatar / PHCC medical statistic
  // 2 Relevant prayer
  // 3 Qatar / Arab-region medical news
  // 4 Positive safe-practice advice
  // 5 Qatar / PHCC medical statistic
  // 6 Abu Nakhla identity animation
  // 7 Greeting (brief, different positive line)
  // 8 Qatar / Arab-region medical news
  // 9 Current QNA / Al Jazeera headline
  // 10 Qatar / PHCC medical statistic
  // 11 Positive doctor encouragement
  // 12 Qatar / Arab-region medical news
  // 13 NHS global/relevant health item
  // 14 Abu Nakhla identity animation
  // then repeat.
  // This makes public-health statistics and medical news much more visi
